 system_prepare_action 
var RPC = window._rpc = (window._rpc || {nextSeq: 1});
window[name] = function() {
  var seq = RPC.nextSeq++;
  var promise = new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
RPC[seq] = {
  resolve: resolve,
  reject: reject,
  window.external.invoke(JSON.stringify({
id: seq,
method: name,
params: Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments),
  return promise;
})()(() => {
let sequence = 0; const pending = new Map();
window.finishNativeRPC = (id, result) => { const item=pending.get(id); if(item){pending.delete(id);item.resolve(result);} };
window.rpcInvoke = (action,payload) => new Promise((resolve,reject) => {
const id=++sequence; pending.set(id,{resolve,reject});
try { window.rpcStart(id,action,payload); } catch (error) { pending.delete(id); reject(error); }

  <script>
    class SpringTracker {
      constructor(pos = 0, stiffness = 320, damping = 28) {
        this.position = pos;
        this.velocity = 0;
        this.target = pos;
        this.stiffness = stiffness;
        this.damping = damping;
      setTarget(newTarget) { this.target = newTarget; }
      advance(dt) {
        const clampedDt = Math.min(Math.max(dt, 0.001), 0.05);
        const accel = -this.stiffness * (this.position - this.target) - this.damping * this.velocity;
        this.velocity += accel * clampedDt;
        this.position += this.velocity * clampedDt;
        return this.position;
    function initAmbientParticles() {
      const canvas = document.getElementById('harmonyCanvas');
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      const resize = () => {
        const scale = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        canvas.width = Math.floor(window.innerWidth * scale);
        canvas.height = Math.floor(window.innerHeight * scale);
        canvas.style.width = window.innerWidth + 'px';
        canvas.style.height = window.innerHeight + 'px';
        ctx.setTransform(scale, 0, 0, scale, 0, 0);
      };
      resize();
      window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
      const particles = Array.from({ length: 24 }, () => ({
        x: Math.random() * window.innerWidth,
        y: Math.random() * window.innerHeight,
        vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.14,
        vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.14,
        radius: Math.random() * 1.2 + 0.4,
        alpha: Math.random() * 0.18 + 0.05
      }));
      function render() {
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        particles.forEach(p => {
          p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy;
          if (p.x < 0 || p.x > window.innerWidth) p.vx *= -1;
          if (p.y < 0 || p.y > window.innerHeight) p.vy *= -1;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(133, 172, 211, ${p.alpha})`;
          ctx.fill();
        });
        requestAnimationFrame(render);
      render();
    const menuParticleColors = {
      sysprops: '#79c7ff', task: '#6ea8ff', service: '#8fb7ff', startup: '#8fd3c7',
      cleanup: '#a7d58a', settings: '#c5b6ff', speedtest: '#62c7d9', diskmanager: '#e0b66d',
      uninstaller: '#e59b9b', popupblocker: '#d0a5e8', fileshredder: '#ef9e78', virusscan: '#f07d86'
    const menuBursts = [];
    function initMenuParticles() {
      const canvas = document.getElementById('menuParticles');
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      const resize = () => {
        const scale = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        canvas.width = Math.floor(window.innerWidth * scale);
        canvas.height = Math.floor(window.innerHeight * scale);
        canvas.style.width = window.innerWidth + 'px';
        canvas.style.height = window.innerHeight + 'px';
        ctx.setTransform(scale, 0, 0, scale, 0, 0);
      };
      resize();
      window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
      let previousNow = performance.now();
      function render(now) {
        const dt = Math.min(Math.max((now - previousNow) / 1000, 0.001), 0.05);
        previousNow = now;
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        for (let i = menuBursts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
          const burst = menuBursts[i];
          const age = (now - burst.started) / 1000;
          burst.items.forEach(p => {
            p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt;
            const drag = Math.pow(0.985, dt * 60);
            p.vx *= drag; p.vy *= drag;
            const life = Math.max(0, 1 - age / p.duration);
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.radius * (0.7 + life * 0.5), 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fillStyle = p.color + Math.round(life * 220).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
            ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
            ctx.shadowColor = p.color;
            ctx.fill();
          });
          if (age > 0.9) menuBursts.splice(i, 1);
        }
        requestAnimationFrame(render);
      requestAnimationFrame(render);
    function burstFromMenu(subId, element) {
      if (!element) return;
      const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
      const originX = rect.right - 2;
      const originY = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
      const targetY = window.innerHeight * 0.48;
      const color = menuParticleColors[subId] || '#79c7ff';
      const items = Array.from({ length: 26 }, (_, index) => {
        const spread = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
        return {
          x: originX + Math.random() * 4,
          y: originY + spread * rect.height * 0.5,
          vx: 220 + Math.random() * 260,
          vy: ((targetY - originY) * 0.9) + spread * 170,
          radius: index % 5 === 0 ? 2.4 : 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.3,
          duration: 0.55 + Math.random() * 0.3,
          color
        };
      });
      menuBursts.push({ started: performance.now(), items });

    let lastMenuOrigin = null;

    const categoryViews = {

    function setPickerCategory(category, visible = transitionPhase === 'picker') {
      const picker = document.getElementById('functionPicker');
      const title = document.getElementById('functionPickerTitle');
      if (!picker) return;
      picker.classList.toggle('visible', visible && category !== 'sysprops');
      title.textContent = category === 'tool' ? '
      document.querySelectorAll('.function-tile').forEach(tile => {

      });
    function setTransitionPhase(phase, token) {
      if (token !== transitionToken) return false;
      transitionPhase = phase;
      return true;
    function rectFor(element) {
      const r = element?.getBoundingClientRect();
      return r && r.width > 0 && r.height > 0 ? r : null;
    const activeTransitionAnimations = new Set();
    const transitionTimers = new Set();
    const pendingLeaves = [];
    function later(fn, ms) {
      const id = setTimeout(() => { transitionTimers.delete(id); fn(); }, ms);
      transitionTimers.add(id);
      return id;
    function clearTransitionTimers() {
      transitionTimers.forEach(id => clearTimeout(id));
      transitionTimers.clear();
      pendingLeaves.forEach(item => {
        clearTimeout(item.id);
        item.el.classList.remove('active', 'visible', 'leaving');
        item.resolve();
      });
      pendingLeaves.length = 0;
      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-cat-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('burst'));
    function resetContentStyle(el) {
      if (!el) return;
      el.getAnimations().forEach(a => { try { a.cancel(); } catch (_) {} });
      ['transform', 'opacity', 'clip-path', 'transform-origin', 'animation'].forEach(p => el.style.removeProperty(p));
    function cancelActiveTransitions() {
      activeTransitionAnimations.forEach(a => { try { a.cancel(); } catch (_) {} });
      activeTransitionAnimations.clear();
      clearTransitionTimers();
      document.querySelectorAll('.page-view, .function-picker').forEach(resetContentStyle);
      document.getElementById('mainStage')?.classList.remove('morph-active');
    /* ===== 
 Napcat/webui/app.js 
 goto() 
 --burst-y
 pageIn 
 --stagger-i 
 childIn
 navBurst 
 --burst-dx / --burst-mid
    function napcatEnter(showEl, originEl, token) {
      if (!showEl) return;
      if (token != null && token !== transitionToken) return;
      const cls = showEl.classList.contains('function-picker') ? 'visible' : 'active';
      showEl.classList.remove('leaving');
      showEl.classList.add(cls);
      const stage = document.getElementById('mainStage');
      const cr = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
      const er = showEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      let burstY = 42, burstX = 0, burstDx = -78, burstMid = 8;
      if (originEl) {
        const r = originEl.getBoundingClientRect();
        const padTop = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(stage).paddingTop) || 0;
        burstY = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.round(r.top + r.height / 2 - cr.top - padTop + stage.scrollTop), Math.max(stage.clientHeight - 20, 0)));
        burstX = Math.round(r.left - er.left);
        burstDx = Math.max(-260, Math.min(160, Math.round(r.left - er.left) - 18));
        burstMid = Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, Math.round(-burstDx * 0.1)));
      showEl.style.setProperty('--burst-y', burstY + 'px');
      showEl.style.setProperty('--burst-x', burstX + 'px');
      showEl.style.setProperty('--burst-dx', burstDx + 'px');
      showEl.style.setProperty('--burst-mid', burstMid + 'px');
      // navBurst
      if (originEl && originEl.classList.contains('nav-cat-btn')) {
        originEl.classList.remove('burst'); void originEl.offsetWidth; originEl.classList.add('burst');
        later(() => originEl.classList.remove('burst'), 460);
      // 
      showEl.style.animation = 'none'; void showEl.offsetWidth; showEl.style.animation = '';
      // 
 stagger 
      Array.from(showEl.children).forEach((el, i) => {
        el.style.setProperty('--stagger-i', Math.min(i, 6));
        el.style.animation = 'none'; void el.offsetWidth; el.style.animation = '';
      });
    function napcatLeave(leaveEl, originEl) {
      if (!leaveEl) return Promise.resolve();
      const stage = document.getElementById('mainStage');
      const er = leaveEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (originEl) {
        const r = originEl.getBoundingClientRect();
        const cr = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
        const padTop = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(stage).paddingTop) || 0;
        const by = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.round(r.top + r.height / 2 - cr.top - padTop + stage.scrollTop), Math.max(stage.clientHeight - 20, 0)));
        leaveEl.style.setProperty('--burst-y', by + 'px');
        leaveEl.style.setProperty('--burst-x', Math.round(r.left - er.left) + 'px');
      leaveEl.classList.add('leaving');
      return new Promise(resolve => {
        const item = { el: leaveEl, resolve };
        item.id = setTimeout(() => {
          const i = pendingLeaves.indexOf(item); if (i >= 0) pendingLeaves.splice(i, 1);
          leaveEl.classList.remove('active', 'visible', 'leaving');
          resolve();
        }, 140);
        pendingLeaves.push(item);
      });
    async function runTransition(target, originEl, token, fromSubView, fromPage, fromCategory) {
      const picker = document.getElementById('functionPicker');
      const stage = document.getElementById('mainStage');
      const current = document.getElementById('view-' + fromSubView);
      if (token !== transitionToken) return false;
      stage.classList.add('morph-active');
      if (fromSubView === 'fileshredder') syncNativeDropZone(false);
      const currentId = 'view-' + fromSubView;
      const targetId = target ? 'view-' + target : null;
      // 
      document.querySelectorAll('.page-view').forEach(v => {
        if (v.id !== currentId && v.id !== targetId && v.classList.contains('active')) {
          v.classList.remove('active'); v.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        }
      });
      // 
sysprops 
      if (fromPage && current && current.classList.contains('active')) {
        setTransitionPhase('returning-picker', token);
        setPickerCategory(fromCategory, true);
        const tile = document.querySelector(`.function-tile[data-view="${fromSubView}"]`);
        const origin = tile || document.querySelector(`.nav-cat-btn[data-cat="${fromCategory}"]`);
        await napcatLeave(current, origin);
        if (token !== transitionToken) return false;
        current.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      if (target === null) {
        // 
        setTransitionPhase('collapsing-sidebar', token);
        const oldBtn = document.querySelector(`.nav-cat-btn[data-cat="${fromCategory}"]`);
        setPickerCategory(fromCategory, true);
        if (picker.classList.contains('visible')) await napcatLeave(picker, oldBtn);
        if (token !== transitionToken) return false;
        setTransitionPhase('expanding-target', token);
        setPickerCategory(currentCategory, true);
        napcatEnter(picker, originEl || oldBtn, token);
        if (token !== transitionToken) return false;
        later(() => stage.classList.remove('morph-active'), 480);
        transitionPhase = 'picker';
        return true;
      // 
sysprops 
      if (!fromPage && target !== 'sysprops') setPickerCategory(currentCategory, true);
      const targetTile = document.querySelector(`.function-tile[data-view="${target}"]`);
      const origin = targetTile || originEl || document.querySelector(`.nav-cat-btn[data-cat="${currentCategory}"]`);
      if (picker.classList.contains('visible')) napcatLeave(picker, origin); // 
      document.querySelectorAll('.page-view').forEach(view => { view.classList.remove('active'); view.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); });
      const targetPage = document.getElementById('view-' + target);
      targetPage.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
      setTransitionPhase('expanding-target', token);
      napcatEnter(targetPage, origin, token);
      if (token !== transitionToken) return false;
      later(() => stage.classList.remove('morph-active'), 480);
      transitionPhase = 'page';
      currentSubView = target;
      targetTile?.classList.add('active');
      if (target === 'fileshredder') syncNativeDropZone(true);
      loadViewData(target);
      return true;
    function onSwitchCategory(targetCat) {
      if (!categoryViews[targetCat]) return;
      const token = ++transitionToken;
      cancelActiveTransitions();
      const navBtn = document.querySelector(`.nav-cat-btn[data-cat="${targetCat}"]`);
      const fromSubView = currentSubView;
      const fromCategory = currentCategory;
      const fromPage = transitionPhase === 'page' && document.getElementById('view-' + fromSubView)?.classList.contains('active');
      currentCategory = targetCat;
      callApi('set_context', { category: targetCat });
      updateSidebarCategoryDOM(true);
      const target = targetCat === 'sysprops' ? 'sysprops' : null;
      const nextSubView = categoryDefaults[targetCat];
      runTransition(target, navBtn, token, fromSubView, fromPage, fromCategory)
        .then(done => { if (done && token === transitionToken) currentSubView = nextSubView; })
        .catch(() => {});
    function onSwitchSubView(subId) {
      if (!(categoryViews[currentCategory] || []).includes(subId)) return;
      const token = ++transitionToken;
      cancelActiveTransitions();
      const fromSubView = currentSubView;
      const fromCategory = currentCategory;
      const fromPage = transitionPhase === 'page' && document.getElementById('view-' + fromSubView)?.classList.contains('active');
      if (!fromPage) setPickerCategory(currentCategory, true);
      runTransition(subId, null, token, fromSubView, fromPage, fromCategory).catch(() => {});
    function updateSidebarCategoryDOM(skipStateReset = false) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-cat-btn').forEach(btn => btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.cat === currentCategory));
      const assistant = document.getElementById('aiAssistant');
      assistant?.classList.toggle('system-visible', currentCategory === 'system');
      if (currentCategory !== 'system') assistant?.classList.remove('open');
      if (skipStateReset) return;
      transitionPhase = currentCategory === 'sysprops' ? 'page' : 'picker';
      setPickerCategory(currentCategory, currentCategory !== 'sysprops');
    async function callApi(action, data = {}) {
      if (window.rpcInvoke) {
        try {
          const res = await window.rpcInvoke(action, JSON.stringify(data));
          return JSON.parse(res);
        } catch (e) {
          console.error(e);
        }
      return null;

      else if (id === 'diskmanager') loadDisks();
      else if (id === 'uninstaller') loadSoftware();
      else if (id === 'popupblocker') loadPopupData();

"]}, {"id": "cp-remote-settings", "title": "
", "category": "
", "description": "
", "target": "control.exe /name Microsoft.System", "keywords": ["
", "remote"]}, {"id": "cp-performance-options", "title": "
", "category": "
", "description": "
", "target": "control.exe /name Microsoft.System", "keywords": ["
    let systemSettingsCache = [];
    let systemSettingMatches = new Set();
    async function loadSystemSettings() {
      const list = await callApi('get_system_settings');
      // 
      systemSettingsCache = (Array.isArray(list) && list.length >= BUILTIN_SETTINGS.length)
        ? list : BUILTIN_SETTINGS;
      renderSystemSettings(document.getElementById('systemSettingsSearch')?.value || '');
    function renderSystemSettings(query = '') {
      const box = document.getElementById('systemSettingsList');
      if (!box) return;
      const normalized = String(query || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const list = systemSettingsCache.filter(item => {
        if (!normalized) return true;
        return [item.title, item.category, item.description, ...(item.keywords || [])].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(normalized);
      });
      box.replaceChildren();
      const groups = new Map();
      list.forEach(item => {
        if (!groups.has(item.category)) groups.set(item.category, []);
        groups.get(item.category).push(item);
      });
      groups.forEach((items, category) => {
        const head = document.createElement('div');
        head.className = 'settings-group-head';
        head.textContent = `${category} 
 ${items.length} 
        box.appendChild(head);
        items.forEach(item => {
          const card = document.createElement('div');
          card.className = 'setting-item';
          if (systemSettingMatches.size && systemSettingMatches.has(item.id)) card.classList.add('highlighted');
          else if (systemSettingMatches.size) card.classList.add('dimmed');
          card.dataset.settingId = item.id;
          card.title = item.target;
          card.onclick = () => openWinSetting(item.id);
          const title = document.createElement('div'); title.className = 'setting-item-title'; title.textContent = item.title;
          const cat = document.createElement('div'); cat.className = 'setting-item-category'; cat.textContent = item.category;
          const desc = document.createElement('div'); desc.className = 'setting-item-description'; desc.textContent = item.description;
          card.append(title, cat, desc); box.appendChild(card);
        });
      });
      const total = document.getElementById('settingsTotal');
      if (total) total.textContent = systemSettingsCache.length;
      const hint = document.getElementById('systemSettingsHint');
      if (hint) hint.textContent = systemSettingMatches.size
        ? `AI 
 ${systemSettingMatches.size} 
        : `

    function filterSystemSettings(value) { renderSystemSettings(value); }
    function localResolveSettings(prompt) {
      const p = String(prompt || '').toLowerCase();
      if (!p) return [];
      return BUILTIN_SETTINGS.map(item => {
        let score = 0;
        if (p.includes(item.id.toLowerCase())) score += 8;
        if (p.includes(item.title.toLowerCase())) score += 6;
        if (p.includes(item.category.toLowerCase())) score += 2;
        (item.keywords || []).forEach(k => { if (k && p.includes(String(k).toLowerCase())) score += 3; });
        return { item, score };
      }).filter(x => x.score > 0).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, 8).map(x => x.item.id);
    async function highlightSystemSettings(prompt) {
      const list = await callApi('resolve_system_setting_intent', { prompt });
      const ids = (Array.isArray(list) && list.length) ? list.slice(0, 8).map(item => item.id) : localResolveSettings(prompt);
      systemSettingMatches = new Set(ids);
      renderSystemSettings(document.getElementById('systemSettingsSearch')?.value || '');
      return list;

    const shredQueue = [];
    async function pickShredFiles() {
      const native = await callApi('pick_files');
      if (window.rpcInvoke) {
        if (native?.paths?.length) { native.paths.forEach(path => addShredPath(path)); renderShredQueue(); }
        return;
      document.getElementById('shredFileInput').click();
    function addShredPath(path, size = 0, name = '') {
      const normalized = String(path || '').trim();
      if (!normalized) return false;
      const fileName = name || normalized.split(/[\\/]/).pop() || normalized;
      if (!shredQueue.some(item => item.path === normalized)) shredQueue.push({ name: fileName, size: Number(size) || 0, path: normalized });
      return true;
    function handleShredFiles(files) {
      for (const file of files || []) {
        if (file?.name && !shredQueue.some(item => !item.path && item.name === file.name && item.size === file.size)) shredQueue.push({ name: file.name, size: Number(file.size) || 0, path: '' });
      renderShredQueue();
    window.onNativeFilesDropped = function(paths) {
      (Array.isArray(paths) ? paths : []).forEach(path => addShredPath(path));
      renderShredQueue();
    function handleShredDrop(event) {
      const files = event.dataTransfer?.files || [];
      handleShredFiles(files);
      if (!files.length) {
        const uriText = event.dataTransfer?.getData('text/uri-list') || event.dataTransfer?.getData('text/plain') || '';
        uriText.split(/\r?\n/).map(value => value.trim()).filter(value => value && !value.startsWith('#')).forEach(value => addShredPath(value.replace(/^file:\/\//, '')));
        renderShredQueue();
    function renderShredQueue() {
      const tbody = document.getElementById('shredQueueBody');
      tbody.replaceChildren();
      shredQueue.forEach((file, index) => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        [file.path || file.name, `${(file.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`, file.path ? '
'].forEach((value, col) => { const td = document.createElement('td'); td.textContent = value; if (col === 2) td.id = `shred-state-${index}`; tr.appendChild(td); });
        tbody.appendChild(tr);
      });

    const dropZone = document.getElementById('shredDropZone');

      ['dragleave', 'drop'].forEach(type => dropZone.addEventListener(type, e => { e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.remove('dragover'); }));

    let nativeDropSyncTimer = 0;
    function syncNativeDropZone(visible) {
      const zone = document.getElementById('shredDropZone');
      if (!zone) return;
      if (!visible) {
        callApi('native_drop_zone', { visible: false });
        return;
      const rect = zone.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return;
      const payload = { visible: true, x: rect.left, y: rect.top, width: rect.width, height: rect.height, scale: window.devicePixelRatio || 1 };
      clearTimeout(nativeDropSyncTimer);
      nativeDropSyncTimer = setTimeout(() => callApi('native_drop_zone', payload), 0);
    let aiSuppressClick = false;
    function toggleAiAssistant() {
      if (aiSuppressClick) { aiSuppressClick = false; return; }

    function applyAiPlacement(clientX, clientY, grabDX, grabDY) {
      const a = document.getElementById('aiAssistant');
      if (!a) return;
      const vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
      const rect = a.getBoundingClientRect();
      const w = rect.width, h = rect.height;
      // 
      // 
      let left = clientX - grabDX;
      let top = clientY - grabDY;
      left = Math.max(8, Math.min(left, vw - w - 8));
      top = Math.max(8, Math.min(top, vh - h - 8));
      // 
 left/right
top/bottom 
      const ax = (left + w / 2) <= vw / 2 ? 'left' : 'right';
      const ay = (top + h / 2) <= vh / 2 ? 'top' : 'bottom';
      const x = ax === 'left' ? left : vw - (left + w);
      const y = ay === 'top' ? top : vh - (top + h);
      a.dataset.ax = ax; a.dataset.ay = ay;
      a.style.setProperty('--ai-x', Math.max(8, Math.round(x)) + 'px');
      a.style.setProperty('--ai-y', Math.max(8, Math.round(y)) + 'px');
    function initAiAnchor() {
      const a = document.getElementById('aiAssistant');
      if (!a || a.dataset.ax) return;
      a.dataset.ax = 'right'; a.dataset.ay = 'bottom';
      a.style.setProperty('--ai-x', '24px');
      a.style.setProperty('--ai-y', '24px');
    function initAiDrag() {
      const orb = document.getElementById('aiOrb');
      const panel = document.querySelector('.ai-panel');
      const assistant = document.getElementById('aiAssistant');
      if (!orb || !assistant || assistant.dataset.dragReady) return;
      assistant.dataset.dragReady = '1';
      let bar = panel?.querySelector('.ai-drag-bar');
      if (!bar && panel) {
        bar = document.createElement('div');
        bar.className = 'ai-drag-bar';
        bar.textContent = '
        panel.insertBefore(bar, panel.querySelector('.ai-input-row'));
      let drag = null;
      const start = e => {
        const r = assistant.getBoundingClientRect();
        drag = { id: e.pointerId, sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY,
                 grabDX: e.clientX - r.left, grabDY: e.clientY - r.top, moved: false };
        try { e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
      };
      const move = e => {
        if (!drag || drag.id !== e.pointerId) return;
        if (Math.abs(e.clientX - drag.sx) > 4 || Math.abs(e.clientY - drag.sy) > 4) drag.moved = true;
        if (drag.moved) applyAiPlacement(e.clientX, e.clientY, drag.grabDX, drag.grabDY);
      };
      const end = e => {
        if (!drag || drag.id !== e.pointerId) return;
        if (drag.moved) aiSuppressClick = true;
        drag = null;
      };
      [orb, bar].filter(Boolean).forEach(src => {
        src.addEventListener('pointerdown', start);
        src.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
        src.addEventListener('pointerup', end);
        src.addEventListener('pointercancel', end);
      });
    function buildDecisionCard(proposal, settingId, message) {
      const item = BUILTIN_SETTINGS.find(x => x.id === settingId) || null;
      const title = (proposal && proposal.settingTitle) || item?.title || proposal?.summary || '
      const category = (proposal && proposal.settingCategory) || item?.category || '';
      const desc = (proposal && proposal.settingDescription) || item?.description || '';
      const target = (proposal && proposal.settingTarget) || item?.target || '';
      const card = document.createElement('div');
      card.className = 'ai-decision';
      const head = document.createElement('div'); head.className = 'ai-decision-head'; head.textContent = '
      const t = document.createElement('div'); t.className = 'ai-decision-title'; t.textContent = title;
      card.append(head, t);
      if (category || target) {
        const m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'ai-decision-meta';
        m.textContent = [category, target].filter(Boolean).join(' 
 '); card.appendChild(m);
      if (desc) { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'ai-decision-desc'; d.textContent = desc; card.appendChild(d); }
      const actions = document.createElement('div'); actions.className = 'ai-decision-actions';
      const guide = document.createElement('button'); guide.className = 'btn-guide'; guide.textContent = '
      guide.onclick = () => guideToSetting(settingId || item?.id);
      actions.appendChild(guide);
      if (proposal && proposal.id) {
        const exec = document.createElement('button'); exec.className = 'btn-exec'; exec.textContent = '
        exec.onclick = async () => {
          exec.disabled = true; exec.textContent = '
          const outcome = await callApi('system_ai_execute', { scope: 'system', id: proposal.id });
          exec.textContent = outcome?.success === false ? '
          const note = document.createElement('div'); note.className = 'ai-decision-desc';
          note.textContent = outcome?.success === false ? (outcome.message || '
') : (outcome?.message || '
          card.appendChild(note);
        };
        actions.appendChild(exec);
      card.appendChild(actions);
      return card;

    let aiRequestToken = 0;
    async function sendAiMessage() {
      const input = document.getElementById('aiInput');
      const reply = document.getElementById('aiReply');
      const message = input.value.trim();
      if (!message) return;
      input.value = '';
      reply.replaceChildren();
      const status = document.createElement('div');
      status.textContent = '
      reply.appendChild(status);
      if (currentCategory !== 'system') { status.textContent = 'AI 
'; return; }
      const myToken = ++aiRequestToken;
      const timeout = new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(() => resolve({ success: false, __timeout: true, message: 'AI 
' }), 35000));
      let result = null;
      try {
        result = await Promise.race([
          callApi('ai_chat', { prompt: message, scope: 'system', messages: [{ role: 'system', content: '
 Windows 
' }] }),
          timeout
        ]);
      } catch (e) { result = null; }
      if (myToken !== aiRequestToken) return; // 
      reply.replaceChildren();
      if (!result || result.success === false) {
        const err = document.createElement('div');
        err.className = 'ai-error';
        err.textContent = result?.message || 'AI 
        reply.appendChild(err);
        return;
      const content = result.reply || result.data?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || result.message;
      if (content && typeof content === 'string' && content.trim()) {
        const t = document.createElement('div'); t.textContent = content; reply.appendChild(t);
      const proposals = Array.isArray(result.pendingActions) ? result.pendingActions : [];
      if (proposals.length) {
        proposals.forEach(p => {
          const sid = p.settingId || (p.args && (p.args.target || p.args.page || p.args.id)) || localResolveSettings(message)[0] || null;
          reply.appendChild(buildDecisionCard(p, sid, message));
        });
      } else {
        const ids = localResolveSettings(message);
        reply.appendChild(buildDecisionCard(null, ids[0] || null, message));
    function backToPicker() {
      if (currentCategory === 'sysprops' || transitionPhase !== 'page') return;
      const token = ++transitionToken;
      cancelActiveTransitions();
      const fromSubView = currentSubView;
      const current = document.getElementById('view-' + fromSubView);
      const picker = document.getElementById('functionPicker');
      const stage = document.getElementById('mainStage');
      if (!current) return;
      const tile = document.querySelector(`.function-tile[data-view="${fromSubView}"]`);
      const origin = tile || document.querySelector(`.nav-cat-btn[data-cat="${currentCategory}"]`);
      stage.classList.add('morph-active');
      if (fromSubView === 'fileshredder') syncNativeDropZone(false);
      setPickerCategory(currentCategory, true);
      (async () => {
        await napcatLeave(current, origin);
        if (token !== transitionToken) return;
        napcatEnter(picker, origin, token);
        later(() => stage.classList.remove('morph-active'), 480);
        transitionPhase = 'picker';
        currentSubView = categoryDefaults[currentCategory];
      })();
    function injectBackButtons() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.page-view').forEach(view => {
        if (view.dataset.category === 'sysprops') return; // 
        const header = view.querySelector('.view-header');
        if (!header || header.querySelector('.back-btn')) return;
        const btn = document.createElement('button');
        btn.className = 'btn-action secondary back-btn';
        btn.textContent = '
        btn.onclick = backToPicker;
        header.insertBefore(btn, header.firstChild);
      });

    function cancelVirusScan() {
      callApi('cancel_virus_scan').then(result => {
        document.getElementById('virusStatusTitle').textContent = result?.message || '
      });
    window.onVirusScanProgress = function(pct, path, count, scanned) {
      const fill = document.getElementById('virusProgressFill');
      fill.style.width = pct < 0 ? '12%' : pct + '%';
      document.getElementById('virusScanPath').textContent = pct < 0 ? `
 ${scanned || count || 0} 
${path || ''}` : '
: ' + path;
    window.onVirusScanComplete = function(threats, summary) {
      const detail = summary && typeof summary === 'object' ? `
${summary.scanned || 0} 
 ${threats.length} 
 ${summary.errors || 0}${summary.cancelled ? '
' : ''}${summary.truncated ? '
' : ''}` : '
      document.getElementById('virusStatusTitle').textContent = detail;
      document.getElementById('virusThreatsCount').textContent = threats.length;
      const tbody = document.querySelector('#tableThreats tbody');
      tbody.innerHTML = '';
      threats.forEach(t => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = `
          <td><span class="badge critical">${t.level}</span></td>
          <td><strong>${t.threatName}</strong></td>
          <td>${t.path} (${t.description})</td>
          <td><button class="btn-action secondary" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:11px;" onclick="quarantineThreat('${encodeURIComponent(t.path)}', this)">
</button></td>
        `;
        tbody.appendChild(tr);
      });
    async function loadQuarantine() {
      const items = await callApi('list_quarantine') || [];
      const panel = document.getElementById('quarantinePanel');
      const tbody = document.querySelector('#tableQuarantine tbody');
      panel.hidden = false; tbody.replaceChildren();
      if (!items.length) { const tr = document.createElement('tr'); const td = document.createElement('td'); td.colSpan = 4; td.textContent = '
'; tr.appendChild(td); tbody.appendChild(tr); return; }
      items.forEach(item => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        [item.id || item.digest || '
', item.originalPath || item.path || '
', item.time || item.timestamp || '
'].forEach(value => { const td = document.createElement('td'); td.textContent = value; tr.appendChild(td); });
        const action = document.createElement('button'); action.className = 'btn-action secondary'; action.textContent = '
'; action.onclick = async () => { if (!confirm('
')) return; action.disabled = true; const result = await callApi('restore_quarantine', { id: item.id || item.digest }); action.textContent = result?.message || (result?.success ? '
        const td = document.createElement('td'); td.appendChild(action); tr.appendChild(td); tbody.appendChild(tr);
      });
    async function quarantineThreat(path, btn) {
      const result = await callApi('quarantine_threat', { path: decodeURIComponent(path) });
      btn.textContent = result?.success ? '
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.style.color = result?.success ? 'var(--system-success)' : 'var(--system-critical)';
    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      initAmbientParticles();
      initMenuParticles();

      document.querySelectorAll('.page-view').forEach(view => view.setAttribute('aria-hidden', view.id !== 'view-sysprops' ? 'true' : 'false'));
      initAiAnchor();
      initAiDrag();

      window.addEventListener('scroll', () => { if (currentSubView === 'fileshredder' && transitionPhase === 'page') syncNativeDropZone(true); }, true);
      callApi('set_context', { category: currentCategory });
      // 
      loadSystemSettings();
  </script>
